    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL (no SPON): 9.22 (+50.6%)\\n- SPON Sparse PPL: 8.99 (+46.9%)\\n- **SPON recovery: 7.3%** (0.23 PPL improvement | 0.12 | 0.23 | +0.11 |\\n| mean_bias_norm | 0.041 | 0.053 | +30% |\\n| spon_sparse_ppl | 9.10 | 8.99 | -0.11 |\\n\\n### 结论: **Negative** (recovery 7.3% < 10% 阈值)\\n消除 seq_len mismatch 后 SPON recovery 从 3.8% 提升到 7.3%，说明 mismatch 确实是部分原因，但即使在匹配条件下 static bias 仍只能恢复不到 10% 的稀疏化误差。确认 D009 \\\"KL subsumes SPON\\\" reframe：KL predictor 的 per-token mask 已经很好地适应了重要 neuron，残余误差不是简单的 additive bias 能补偿的。\", \"parent\": \"spon_training\"}, \"bce_isocompute_v2_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:01:43.104Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-23T00:11:35.391Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"BCE 在 iso-compute (1000 steps) + iso-sparsity (STE reg_weight=2.0 使 sparsity 收敛到 ~0.50) 下是否能缩小与 KL 的 PPL 差距？消除 compute 和 sparsity 两个 confound。\", \"notes\": \"## Eval Results (2026-05-16)\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: 26.76\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: 54.84\\n- sparsity_target: 0.50 (converged correctly, STE fix worked)\\n\\n## Training Summary\\n- 1000 steps, 1048s (1.05s/step), EXIT_CODE=0\\n- BCE loss: 0.6714 → 0.6586 (only 5% better than random 0.693)\\n- Reg loss: 0.0171 → 0.0051 (sparsity converged to 0.50)\\n- reg_weight=2.0, Gumbel-sigmoid STE\\n\\n## Comparison with KL Baseline\\n- KL normalized (50% sparsity, 4-seed mean): teal_sparse_ppl = 12.00 ± 0.07\\n- BCE v2: teal_sparse_ppl = 54.84 — 4.6x worse than KL\\n\\n## Conclusion\\nBCE + STE dramatically underperforms KL at 50% sparsity under iso-compute (1000 steps). BCE treats each neuron as an independent binary classification, losing inter-neuron coordination and output distribution signal. The predictor barely learned meaningful patterns (BCE only 5% better than random). KL's direct output-distribution matching provides much stronger learning signal. BCE is not a viable alternative loss for sparsity predictor training.\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"metrics\": {\"final_loss\": 0.6637, \"final_bce_loss\": 0.6586, \"final_reg_loss\": 0.0051, \"final_sparsity\": 0.502, \"training_steps\": 1000, \"training_time_sec\": 1047.8, \"reg_weight\": 2, \"true_dense_ppl\": 6.12, \"predictor_hard_mask_ppl\": 26.76, \"teal_sparse_ppl\": 54.84, \"eval_seq_len\": 2048, \"eval_dataset\": \"wikitext-2\"}}, \"kl_sparsity70_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:34:18.823Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-23T00:11:33.762Z\", \"metrics\": {\"true_dense_ppl\": 6.12, \"predictor_hard_mask_ppl\": 13.84, \"predictor_teal_sparse_ppl\": 17.91, \"vanilla_teal_sparse_ppl\": 100.11, \"teal_sparse_ppl\": 17.91, \"teal_sparse_ppl_static_baseline\": 17.91, \"model_level_sparsity\": 0.7, \"teal_sparse_note\": \"17.91 = predictor-based TEAL (teal_global_masks: predictor-gated magnitude scoring + global topk @70%). vanilla TEAL @70% = 100.11. 代码来源: quick_sp70.py L10 imports teal_global_masks, L60 调用. plan_007_all_ppl_fixed.json 的 mode=static_mask_70pct metadata 有误导性.\", \"eval_dataset\": \"wikitext-2\", \"eval_seq_len\": 2048, \"calibration_samples\": 32, \"per_layer_sparsity_range\": [0.508, 0.876]}, \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"notes\": \"## Training Analysis (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Training Summary\\n- 1000/1000 steps completed, EXIT_CODE=0\\n- Sparsity converged to ~0.688 (target 0.70, within 0.65-0.75 success range)\\n- Lambda hit cap 1000 by step 10, tau annealed 0.999 → 0.100\\n- KL loss: 1.18 → 0.61\\n\\n### PPL Evaluation Results\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: **13.84** (per-token adaptive mask)\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: **17.91** (static global topk, FIXED via D017)\\n- model_level_sparsity: 0.699\\n\\n### D017 Bug Fix (2026-05-15)\\nOriginal teal_sparse_ppl=136,069 was caused by using raw sigmoid scores for global topk mask selection. Sigmoid probabilities lack magnitude information and are poor proxies for static neuron importance. Fix: score neurons by mean(|intermediate| * predictor_hard_mask) — activation magnitude weighted by predictor decisions. Also added per-layer mean normalization (from _global_topk_masks). Result: 136K → 17.91.\", \"hypothesis\": \"KL distillation 在 70% 极端稀疏度 (beyond-critical regime) 下仍能收敛且保持相对于 TEAL 的 PPL 优势——验证方法在高稀疏度下 advantage ratio 是否放大\"}, \"jsd_50pct_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T06:00:24.134Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-23T00:11:32.123Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"JSD (symmetric divergence) balances mode-covering and mode-seeking properties, potentially combining forward KL's PPL advantage with reverse KL's downstream preservation\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"metrics\": {\"ppl_wikitext2\": 12.54, \"vs_forward_kl\": \"+0.75 (+6.4%)\", \"sparsity\": 0.5}}, \"reverse_kl_50pct_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T06:00:23.139Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-23T00:11:30.445Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Reverse KL (mode-seeking, KL(p_sparse||p_dense)) produces different sparsity patterns than forward KL (mode-covering); mode-seeking may concentrate probability mass on fewer but more important neurons, potentially improving downstream at cost of PPL\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"metrics\": {\"ppl_wikitext2\": 12.8, \"vs_forward_kl\": \"+1.01 (+8.6%)\", \"sparsity\": 0.5}}, \"qwen14b_kl30_uniform_eval_4task\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-18T00:31:52.875Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-23T00:11:28.029Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"KL predictor 14B uniform allocation 4-task eval (no GSM8K). 训练正常完成1000步。Compensation带来约9%的per-token PPL改善(27.96→25.44)，但仍远差于KL-only(9.21)，差距2.8倍。TEAL路径不经过comp network，PPL=44.54与BCE-only一致。训练曲线：comp_loss从Phase2开始的0.0053稳定下降至0.0033，说明comp网络在学习但改善空间有限。⚠️ Sparsity mismatch: BCE+comp model_level_sparsity=0.558 vs KL model_level_sparsity=0.498。论文对比时必须标注此差异——BCE+comp 稀疏度更高意味着其 PPL 劣势可能被低估。结论：compensation不能弥补BCE损失函数的根本缺陷——不区分neuron重要性。强化Claim 1：KL {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T17:27:18.117Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:59:29.428Z\", \"notes\": \"## 训练概要\\n1000 steps 正常完成，loss 波动在 0.38-0.47 区间，未有效收敛。Bias norm 从 0→0.041 后在 step 700 饱和。LR cosine+warmup 正常 (1e-5→0)。仅 458K/8B 可训练参数 (0.0057%)。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Per-token sparse PPL (predictor hard mask, no SPON): 9.22 (+50.6%)\\n- Per-token sparse + SPON PPL: 9.10 (+48.7%)\\n- SPON recovery: 仅 3.8% of sparse-vs-dense gap (0.12 PPL improvement)\\n- TEAL global mask sparse PPL (from KL-only eval): 12.08\\n\\n## 结论: **Negative**\\nSPON bias 补偿效果极其微弱，仅恢复 3.8% 的稀疏化误差。可能原因：\\n1. Bias norm 极小 (mean=0.041) 且提前饱和，说明学习容量不足或信号太弱\\n2. 训练 seq_len=128 vs eval seq_len=2048 的 mismatch 可能削弱效果\\n3. Per-token predictor hard mask 本身已经较好 (sparse PPL 9.22 vs TEAL global 12.08)，残余误差可能不是简单的 additive bias 能补偿的\\n\\n## Claim 2 影响\\nSPON + KL predictor 的加性组合改善微不足道。如果要验证 Claim 2，可能需要：(a) 更高表达力的补偿机制替代 static bias，或 (b) 更长 seq_len 训练，或 (c) 在 TEAL global mask 场景下测试 SPON（误差更大，补偿空间更大）。\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"metrics\": {\"true_dense_ppl\": 6.1193, \"predictor_hard_mask_ppl\": 9.2165, \"magnitude_ratio\": 0.1578, \"magnitude_ratio_std\": 0.0088, \"cosine_similarity\": 0.1962, \"cosine_similarity_std\": 0.0268, \"predictor_effect_norm\": 482.65, \"spon_correction_norm\": 76.2, \"interaction_norm\": 147.74, \"num_samples\": 50, \"seq_len\": 2048, \"conclusion_type\": \"weak_subsumption\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\", \"parent\": \"spon_training\"}, \"eval_bce_v3_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:58:48.632Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"GSM8K 生成质量对比：dense vs KL sparse 50% greedy 生成 5-10 样本。判断退化是\\\"推理丧失（重复/无意义文本）\\\"还是\\\"仅数字算错\\\"。\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"## GSM8K 生成质量对比：Dense vs KL Sparse 50% (10 samples)\\n\\n### 结论：推理能力根本性丧失（模式 A），非仅数字算错\\n\\n| 维度 | Dense | Sparse 50% |\\n|------|-------|------------|\\n| 推理连贯性 | 多步推理，逻辑清晰 | 1-3 句后进入无限重复循环 |\\n| 正确答案 | 多数正确 | 0/10 |\\n| 重复退化 | 无 | 10/10 全部出现 |\\n| 拼写质量 | 正常 | 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\", \"parent\": \"lm_eval_kl_s42\"}, \"d030_kl40_uniform\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T05:18:45.733Z\", \"updated_at\":   "task_prompt": "## 任务：分析 perlayer_kl_k16_s456_v2 训练结果\n\n实验已完成训练（进程正常退出），需要提取训练指标。\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. **SSH 到 westd-34887 读取训练日志**：\n   - 日志路径：`/root/runs/distill_sparse_swiglu/perlayer_kl_k16_s456_v2.log`\n   - 用 `ssh_read_only` 读取日志末尾 100 行，提取：\n     - 最终 KL loss（final_kl 或 avg_kl）\n     - 最终 sparsity（avg_sparsity）\n     - 训练步数是否达到 1000\n     - 是否有报错\n     - Wall time\n\n2. **检查 checkpoint 是否存在**：\n   - Checkpoint 路径：`/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/perlayer_kl_k16_s456/`\n   - 用 `ssh_read_only` 检查 `ls -la /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/perlayer_kl_k16_s456/`\n   - 确认有 step_1000 或 final checkpoint\n\n3. **对比同系列实验**：\n   参考数据（K=16 其他 seeds 的训练指标）：\n   - K=16 s42 (perlayer_kl_k16_s42_v3): final_kl=0.1406, avg_kl=0.1582, avg_sparsity=0.286\n   - K=16 s123 (perlayer_kl_k16_s123): avg_kl=0.1617, avg_sparsity=0.287, final_kl=0.1436\n\n4. **产出**：\n   输出一个 JSON 格式的 summary，包含：\n   ```json\n   {\n     \"training_success\": true/false,\n     \"final_kl\": ...,\n     \"avg_kl\": ...,\n     \"avg_sparsity\": ...,\n     \"wall_time_s\": ...,\n     \"steps_completed\": ...,\n     \"checkpoint_path\": \"...\",\n     \"checkpoint_exists\": true/false,\n     \"comparison_note\": \"与 s42/s123 相比是否一致\"\n   }\n   ```\n\n### 服务器信息\n- Server: westd-34887\n- 项目路径: /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/\n",
